32a1(rgyud): @#/ O /rnam par snang mdzad brjod//gang phyir byang sems gsher ba nyid//gsher ba chu yi khams su brjod//chu ni mi bskyod gzug+sa

37a3(rgyud): sha chen shA liM dza zhes brjod//dbang po gnyis sbyor kun du ru//rdo rje 'bo la zhes bshad de//pad ma ka k+ko la zhes zer//kha dog dbye bas phye ba las//rigs 
37a4(rgyud): ni rnam pa lnga ru bshad//'di ni gsang ba'i skad yin no//phyag rgya lnga ni bsdus pa nyid//g.yung mo rdo rje'i rigasu bshad//gar ma de bzhin pad ma'i rigs//
37a5(rgyud): rang 'tshed ma ni rin chen rigs//skyes gnyis de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs//chos ma las kyi rigs nyid de//phyag rgya 'di rnams dngos grub sbyin//'di rnams 
37a6(rgyud): khu ba rdo rjer 'gyur//rtul zhugs can gyis mchod nas btung //rdo rje snying po sems dpa' che//gsang ba'i skad ni mtshar che ba//ngas yis khyod la gang bshad pa//
37a7(rgyud): de rnams thams cad gus pas bzung //kye rdo rje 'dir dbang bskur gang //gsang ba'i skad kyis mi smra ba//de ni dam tshig nyams par ni//'gyur bar 'di la 
37b1(rgyud): the tshom med//'tshe ba dang ni rkun ma dang //gdon dang rim dang dug gis kyang //gal te sangs rgyas gsang ba'i skad//mi gsung na yang 'grongs par 'gyur//
37b2(rgyud): rang gis dam tshig rigs snyed nas//gal te tshig 'di mi smra na//gnas bzhi las skyes rnal 'byor ma//de yi tshe na khro bar byed//kyai rdo rje mkha' 'gro ma dra 
37b3(rgyud): ba'i sdom pa las rgyud thams cad kyi gleng bzhi dang gsang ba'i skad ces bya ba'i////le'u ste gsum pa'o////de nas rdo rje snying po la sogs 
37b4(rgyud): pa'i mkha' 'gro ma rnams the tshom du gyur//yid gnyis su gyur te//bcom ldan 'das rdo rje sems dpa' la 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan the tshom 
37b5(rgyud): bsal du gsol//spyod pa'i le'u las gang bshad//glu dang gar ni dngos bsgrub sbyin//de la bdag gi the tshom mchis//glu ni ci lags gar yang ci//
37b6(rgyud): lha yi le'u las gang gsungs pa//zhe sdang la sogs phyag rgya rnams//de la bdag ni the tshom mchis//gang gi phyag rgya phya rgya ci//sngags kyi le'u 
37b7(rgyud): las gang gsungs pa//bdag med la sogs sa bon nyid//de la bdag ni 'khrul pa 'khrungs//gang gis sa bon sa bon gang //rigs kyi le'u 
38a1(rgyud): @#/ O /las gang bshad pa//bcu drug gnyis kyi bdag nyid rtsa//bcom ldan bdag ni 'khrul gyur pas//de rnams rnam 
38a2(rgyud): dag bshad du gsol//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa//ko l+la i ri T+wi a 'bo lA//mum+mu mang ni (ra)ka k+ko lA//g+ha Na ki pi Ta ho badzra i/ka ru Ne ki

38b1(rgyud): byed||'dis ni sngags kyi bzlas pa nyid//gang du gus pas glu len dang //gang du gus bcas gar byed pa//tshogs kyi bdag pos sngar byas nas//de la dri 
38b2(rgyud): ni 'tshon par bya//sgog pa'i dri ni dang po nyid//de nas yang ni bya rgod dri//ga pur ma la yaM dzaM nyid//ngang pa dang ni bung ba'i sgra//glu yi mtha' nas mnyan 
38b3(rgyud): par bya//ba lang 'tshe yis sgra yang ni//phyi rol tshal du mtshon bya ba//glu yi byin brlabs mtshan nyid do//phyag rgya rtags dang mtshan ma ste//'dis ni rig+sa 
38b4(rgyud): ni mtshon par bya//rigs 'chol bsgom pa'i sbyor ba las//dngos grub med cing bsgrub po 'ang med//zhe sdang phyag rgyas bdag med ma//gti mug phyag 
38b5(rgyud): rgyas rdo rje ma//ser sna'i phyag rgyas dkar mo nyid//chags pas chu ma phyag rgyas gdab//phra dog phyag rgyas mkha' 'gro ma//zhe sdang phyag rgyas puk+ka sI//
38b6(rgyud): gti mug phyag rgyas ri khrod ma//ser sna'i phyag rgyasagadol pa mo//'dod chags phyag rgyas g.yung mo nyid//yang ni dkar mo zhe sdang las//gti mug phyag rgyas 
38b7(rgyud): chom rkun ma//ser sna'i phyag rgyas ro langs ma//'dod chags phyag rgyasag+hasma rI//gti mug phyag rgyas sa spyod ma//'dod chags phyag rgya mkha' spyod ma//
39a1(rgyud): /'dod pas phyag rgyar shes par bya//a li dang po bdag med ma//a li gnyis pa rdo rje ma//a li gsum pa dkar mo nyid//
39a2(rgyud): bzhi pa chu yi rnal 'byor ma//lnga pa rdo rje mkha' 'gro ma//drug pa pu ka sI zhes brjod//bdun pa nyid ni ri khrod ma//brgyad pa sme sha can du brjod//dgu pa yang ni 
39a3(rgyud): g.yung mo nyid//yang ni lnga gnyis dkar mo nyid//bcu gcig chom rkun ma ru brjod//bcu gnyis ro langs ma zhes brjod//bcu gsum pa ni g+hasma rI//
39a4(rgyud): bcu bzhi pa ni sa spyod ma//bcwo lnga pa ni mkha' spyod ma//rnal 'byor ma yis sa bon nyid//rigs kyi le'u las rtsa rnams gang //bcu drug gnyis kyi bdag 
39a5(rgyud): nyid gsungs//rtsa ni gnyis gnyis rnal 'byor ma//re re rnam pa rnams su brjod//rkyang ma ro ma kun 'dar ma//bdag med rnal 'byor ma zhes brjod//
39a6(rgyud): gang phyir bcu drug cha med pas//'bad pas kun gyis lhag ma spang //ci'i slad du zhe na//don gyi bya ba mi byed phyir//bco lnga cha yi bdag nyid kyi//
39a7(rgyud): zla ba byang chub sems su 'gyur//bde ba chen po a la'i gzugs//rnal 'byor ma rnams de'i cha//rdo rje snying pos gsol pa//rnal 'byor ma kun las 
39b1(rgyud): byung ba//lan cig skyes dgu'i rang bzhin nyid//'grib med btung mchog namakha' nyid//ga pur cis nas nges mi spangs//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' 
39b2(rgyud): stsal pa//ji skad smras pa de bzhin no//rdo rje snying pos gsol pa//thabs gang gis byang chub kyi sems bskyed pa lags//bcom ldan 'das kyi bka' stsal 
39b3(rgyud): pa//dkyil 'khor 'khor lo'i thabs dang ni//rang byin brlab pa'i rim pas kyang //kun rdzob don dam gzugs can gyis//byang chub sems ni rab tu 
39b4(rgyud): bskyed//kun rdzob kun d+ha lta bu nyid//don dam bde ba'i gzugs can no//bud med kak+ko la bde ba can//e ba~M rnam pa'i rang bzhin du//bde ba bsrung 
39b5(rgyud): ba nyid kyi phyir//bde ba can zhes rab tu bsgrags//sangs rgyas byang chub sems dpa' dang //rdo rje 'dzin pa rnams kyi gnas//'di nyid 'khor ba zhes bya ste//
39b6(rgyud): 'di nyid mya ngan 'das pa nyid//'khor ba spangs nas gzhan du ni//mya ngan 'das pa rtogs mi 'gyur//'khor ba gzugs dang sgra la sogs//'khor ba tshor 
39b7(rgyud): ba la sogs pa//'khor ba dbang po rnams nyid de//'khor ba zhe sdang la sogs pa//'di rnams chos ni mya ngan 'das//rmongs phyir 'khor ba'i gzugs can nyid//
40a1(rgyud): @#/ O /rmongs med 'khor ba dag pas ni//'khor ba mya ngan 'das par 'gyur//byang chub sems ni mya ngan 'das//kun rdzob don dam tshul  
40a2(rgyud): can no//shin tu bzhin bzangs mig yangs ma//gzugs dang lang tshos rnam par brgyan//sngo bsangs dal ma rigs bzangs ma//si h+la ga pur 'byung ba'i gnas//
40a3(rgyud): rang gis dbang bskur kye rdo rje//skra bzangs bsgrubs pa po la dga'//der ni chang yang btung bar bya//de nas rang yang btung ba nyid//rang bzhin don ni rab bsgrub 
40a4(rgyud): phyir//phyi nas phyag rgya rjes chags bya//bo la ka k+ko lar bcug nas//kun du ru byed rtul shugs can//der ni sbyor ba las byung ba'i//ga pur mkhas pas mi 
40a5(rgyud): speng ste//de ni lag tu mi blang zhing //nya phyis dung chos nyid du min//nges par blo dang stobs kyi phyir//mi 'chi lce yis blang ba nyid//ga pur nyid ni bdag med

40b2(rgyud): sbyin pa'i dngos grub 'gyur//gzugs sgra de bzhin dri dang ni//ro dang de bzhin reg bya nyid//chos kyi dbyings kyi rang bzhin yang //shes rab nyid kyis spyad par bya//

41a1(rgyud): @#/ O /bde ba phyag rgya che//rdo rje skye mched thams cad nyid//'dis ni gsang ba'i snyoms 'jug pas//phyi rol gnyis gnyis
